# Max Booster test catalog

Generated by `python3 scripts/testing/generate-catalog.py` from repository source. This index describes what each file does and how to select it; actual pass/fail/skip outcomes are in [the recorded results](../../reports/test-verification/README.md). It does not execute tests or claim production readiness.

The census contains **566 canonical test/operational files**, 55 runner/helper source files, 10 data/documentation fixtures and 71 retained generated/historical test copies.

Commands below run from the repository root unless a different working directory is stated. Run the clean-environment group runner first; a per-file command is a selector, not permission to use the application's real database, private model state, production bucket or provider account. Requirements and risk notes are part of each command's instructions.

Literal titles and Python/Go/Rust definitions are source descriptions. Parameterized tests, loops, subtests and generated suites may produce different runtime counts. Files with no static titles can still be executable assertion scripts. Support files and retained snapshots are listed separately and must not count as passed tests.

| Group | Indexed files |
| --- | ---: |
| Root Vitest unit suites | 277 |
| Root Node contracts and assertion scripts | 89 |
| Python suites and bounded regression scripts | 115 |
| Commerce and beat lifecycle suites | 17 |
| Integration and real browser suites | 26 |
| Rust, Go and imported subsystem suites | 5 |
| Operational, load, provider and hardware checks | 37 |
| Runners, fixtures, helpers and configuration | 55 |

Regenerate after changing or adding tests. `python3 scripts/testing/generate-catalog.py --check` compares source paths, classifications and hashes without executing tests. The recorded Git SHA identifies the base revision; per-file hashes also capture uncommitted test edits present when the inventory was generated.

## Root Vitest unit suites

### [server/pocket-dimension/__tests__/index.test.ts](../../server/pocket-dimension/__tests__/index.test.ts)

PocketDimension compression engine (codec-mesh integration); round-trips a plain write/read using the new codec-mesh container format

Runner: `vitest`. Working directory: `.`.

```sh
env -i PATH="$PATH" LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}" HOME=/tmp LANG=C CI=1 npx vitest run --config vitest.config.ts server/pocket-dimension/__tests__/index.test.ts
```

Requirements: Node.js >=22.12; npm ci.

<details><summary>Source case labels (9; runtime expansion may differ)</summary>

- PocketDimension compression engine (codec-mesh integration)
- round-trips a plain write/read using the new codec-mesh container format
- round-trips arbitrary binary data (not just text) through the new codec
- round-trips data through an encrypted pocket dimension with the new codec
- reads a pre-existing legacy gzip chunk with no container envelope (backward compatibility)
- dedupes identical content within one dimension while still round-tripping
- PocketDimension recovery initialization
- allows a genuinely new pocket only when metadata and index are both absent
- propagates PDIM read failures and remains retryable without writes

</details>

- passes repeat.every to every registered job (BullMQ dashboard visibility)
- creates a BullMQ Worker for the autonomous queue
- starts the worker via run() (deferred via setImmediate)
- is idempotent — second call closes old worker before creating a new one
- content-dispatch repeats every 60 seconds
- analytics repeats every hour
- all prune jobs repeat at least hourly (≥ 3_600_000 ms)
- all jobs have removeOnComplete:true
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — teardown & shutdown
- teardownRepeatableJobs() closes the worker (graceful drain)
- teardownRepeatableJobs() resets isSchedulerLeader to false
- teardownRepeatableJobs() is safe when no worker is running
- closeScheduler() closes both worker and queue
- closeScheduler() is safe to call multiple times
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — isSchedulerLeader()
- returns false before any jobs are set up
- returns false after teardown
- returns false after closeScheduler
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — campaign optimization
- scheduleCampaignOptimization() registers a repeatable job with every:300_000
- scheduleCampaignOptimization() is idempotent for the same campaign
- different campaigns can be scheduled independently
- removeCampaignOptimization() calls removeRepeatableByKey when key is stored
- removeCampaignOptimization() falls back to scanning when key not locally stored
- removeCampaignOptimization() is safe when campaign was never scheduled
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — AUTONOMOUS_QUEUE constant
- exports a non-empty string queue name
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — job execution (campaign)
- worker processor calls autonomousService.runCampaignOptimization with campaignId
- worker processor warns and skips when campaignId is missing from job data
- isSchedulerLeader() returns true during job execution
- isSchedulerLeader() stays true after job completes (within staleness window)
- BullMQ autonomous scheduler — sentinel cleared on add failure
- clears the idempotency sentinel when queue.add throws, allowing retry
- withSchedLock utility (distributedLock — aggregation use)
- exports withSchedLock, acquireLock, releaseLock, withLock
- single-instance mode: always executes fn (no PDIM)
- single-instance mode: does not call SET NX (no Redis needed)
- PDIM mode: executes fn when SET NX returns OK
- PDIM mode: skips fn when SET NX returns null (lock held by another pod)
- PDIM mode: fails closed and skips fn when lock acquisition throws
- PDIM mode: swallows fn() errors without propagating

</details>

<details><summary>Source case labels (10; runtime expansion may differ)</summary>

- Beat Money Loop lifecycle on a disposable PostgreSQL database
- concurrent initialization creates exactly one paused state row
- generates decoded audio, a shorter private preview, awareness artwork, atomic listing and royalty ownership
- a second replica cannot run or orphan an active cycle while its database lock is held
- only a completed marketplace settlement marks sold; replay does not duplicate seller earnings
- zero published posts is reported honestly while retaining the payable listing
- concurrent manual requests cannot overlap and pause clears queued cycles
- listing failure rolls back beat and royalty rows and removes public/audio uploads
- silent audio is rejected before listing even without renderer quality metadata
- renderer failure lists nothing, releases the slot and records backoff

</details>

- durable checkout SQL serializes retries, rolls back order receipts, and fences expired unknown payments
- PostgreSQL migrations and real CommerceRepository settlement/fulfillment
- applies additive 0022/0023 migrations and books one balanced, allocated order atomically
- rolls back source, allocations, journal, revenue, and order status when fulfillment is not ready
- concurrent duplicate settlements serialize on the production advisory lock
- rejects a reused PaymentIntent attached to another order
- database deferred constraint also rejects unbalanced journals at COMMIT
- CommerceEngine executes production payout/refund/reversal workflows against SQL
- reserves against funded payable, idempotently, and rejects over-reservation
- concurrent same-key requests for conflicting payout accounts have one immutable winner
- pays a claimed reservation through the engine's real SQL state transitions
- refund authorization, duplicate intent, partial reversal, and over-refund guardrails
- full refund creates balanced reversal, exposes the debt, then recovers it exactly once
- production commerce webhook inbox deduplicates concurrent duplicate events
- real Stripe test-mode checkout and PaymentIntent settle through production code in disposable SQL

</details>

<details><summary>Source case labels (18; runtime expansion may differ)</summary>

- A. Royalty split ledger
- writes a royalty_transactions row with the correct amount
- increments royalty_splits.total_earned and pending_payout
- does NOT double-credit on a second call (idempotency)
- B. Revenue events
- records a revenue_events row with source='marketplace'
- ON CONFLICT: a second revenue_events insert is a no-op
- C. Sale notifications — confirmed payment idempotency
- processPayment on completed order delivers beat_sold + beat_purchased notifications
- replay — second processPayment call produces no additional notifications
- concurrent calls — only one delivery wins; exactly one notification per recipient
- failure resets notifStatus so the next retry can re-attempt delivery
- no beat_sold notification from initiation-time route send
- D. Content-dispatch — beat-loop posts
- beat-loop post was inserted with _beatMoneyLoop marker and overdue scheduledAt
- runContentDispatch query selects the overdue beat-loop post
- publishToPlatform enqueues a job without throwing
- post transitions to published after successful dispatch

</details>

- 4. POST /api/auth/2fa/setup returns secret and QR code

- 9. login with password alone returns requiresTwoFactor:true when 2FA is enabled
- 10. login with password + valid TOTP code fully authenticates

- 16. POST /api/auth/2fa/validate with a valid TOTP code returns { valid: true }
- 12. POST /api/auth/2fa/disable with wrong password returns 400
- 13. POST /api/auth/2fa/disable with correct password + valid TOTP disables 2FA
- 14. GET /api/auth/2fa/status confirms 2FA is disabled
- 15. logout and re-login works without 2FA code after disable

</details>

### [tests/unit/cache-cross-pod.test.ts](../../tests/unit/cache-cross-pod.test.ts)

Cross-pod cache invalidation — simulation test. ┌─ PDIM pub/sub constraint ──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ PDIM does NOT support pub/sub. PUBLISH returns 0 and SUBSCRIBE returns │

Runner: `vitest`. Working directory: `.`.

Configuration membership: `vitest.config.ts`, `vitest.integration.config.ts`. The file is indexed once here even when more than one runner selects it.

```sh
env -i PATH="$PATH" LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}" HOME=/tmp LANG=C MAXBOOSTER_PYTHON="${MAXBOOSTER_PYTHON:-python3}" node scripts/testing/integration-browser.mjs tests/unit/cache-cross-pod.test.ts
```

Requirements: PostgreSQL server tools and configured MaxCore Python dependencies; The wrapper creates disposable PostgreSQL and a sealed loopback app fixture; see integration-browser.md.

Execution limits: May create/delete fixture accounts and application records; never point at the user's running app.

<details><summary>Source case labels (8; runtime expansion may differ)</summary>

- Cross-pod cache invalidation (PDIM polling simulation)
- PDIM pub/sub is unavailable — pollTick() is the cross-pod coordination mechanism
- pod A invalidateForUser → pod B bust-key check rejects stale L1 entry before poll
- pod A invalidatePattern → pod B pollTick() evicts matching pod B L1 entries
- seq counter increments on invalidation, waking pollers on other pods
- invalidatePattern writes "{pattern}:{timestamp}" event to PDIM list
- invalidateForUser writes userId→bustAt to PDIM hash consumed by other pod pollers
- keeps the bust key monotonic when an older invalidation write completes later

</details>

### [tests/payment-processing/inbound-known-failures.mjs](../../tests/payment-processing/inbound-known-failures.mjs)

Regression assertions for previously confirmed payment-processing defects.

Runner: `support`. Working directory: `.`.

Standalone command: none. Invoked or imported by its owning test runner; do not run as a standalone test.

<details><summary>Source case labels (5; runtime expansion may differ)</summary>

- billing checkout retries reuse the durable session for the same command
- storefront cart retries reuse one session and one set of pending orders
- beat purchase completion verifies the payment before recording a sale
- beat purchase completion grants a sale only with the verified charge amount
- Stripe beat-payment verification rejects unpaid, mispriced, and misattributed intents

</details>
